--[[
    FluentGlass LIQUID  -  Fluent + Liquid Glass (Roblox, hỗ trợ cảm ứng)
    Kiểu: kính xám mờ, mọi khối có viền sáng bo tròn, thanh tìm kiếm, dropdown nổi, hiệu ứng "lỏng".

    local Library = loadstring(readfile("FluentGlass_Liquid.lua"))()
    local Window  = Library:CreateWindow({Title="Example Hub", SubTitle="v1.1.0", Theme="Obsidian White"})
    local Tab     = Window:AddTab("Main", "⌂")
    Tab:AddSection("Tên mục")
    Tab:AddLabel("Nội dung")
    Tab:AddButton("Tên", function() end, "mô tả (tuỳ chọn)")
    Tab:AddToggle("Tên", false, function(v) end, "mô tả")
    Tab:AddSlider("Tên", 0, 100, 50, function(v) end, "mô tả")
    Tab:AddDropdown("Tên", {"A","B"}, "A", function(v) end, "mô tả")
    Tab:AddInput("Tên", "gợi ý", function(text) end, "mô tả")
    (hoặc truyền bảng: Tab:AddToggle({Title=..., Description=..., Default=..., Callback=...}))
    Library:Notify("Tiêu đề", "Nội dung", 4)      -- duration = 0 -> không tự tắt
    Library:SetTheme("Midnight Blue")               -- Obsidian White / Midnight Blue / Crimson Dark / Ember / Neon Crimson
    Library:SetGlass(0..1)                          -- 0 trong suốt ... 1 mờ đục (mặc định 0.5)
    Window:Toggle()  Window:Maximize()  Window:Destroy()
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

local Library = { Windows = {}, GlassLevel = 0.62, Effects = true }

local WHITE = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
local TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(246, 248, 252)
local SUB = Color3.fromRGB(196, 202, 212)

Library.Themes = {
    ["Obsidian White"] = { Tint = Color3.fromRGB(88, 92, 98), Accent = Color3.fromRGB(240, 244, 252) },
    ["Midnight Blue"] = { Tint = Color3.fromRGB(38, 54, 96), Accent = Color3.fromRGB(120, 170, 255) },
    ["Crimson Dark"] = { Tint = Color3.fromRGB(86, 34, 42), Accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 120) },
    ["Ember"] = { Tint = Color3.fromRGB(98, 62, 34), Accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 90) },
    ["Neon Crimson"] = { Tint = Color3.fromRGB(62, 24, 52), Accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 160) },
}
Library.ThemeName = "Obsidian White"
Library.Tint = Library.Themes["Obsidian White"].Tint
Library.Accent = Library.Themes["Obsidian White"].Accent

-- ========== Tiện ích ==========
local GlassItems = {}   -- mọi khung kính (để chỉnh độ kính / theme)
local AccentItems = {}  -- {object, property} dùng màu nhấn
local Irid = {}         -- gradient viền cầu vồng
local iridConn

local function new(class, props, children)
    local o = Instance.new(class)
    if class == "TextLabel" or class == "TextButton" or class == "TextBox" then
        o.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 20, 40)
        o.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.9
    end
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
    for _, c in ipairs(children or {}) do c.Parent = o end
    return o
end

local function tween(o, t, props, style, dir)
    local tw = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
    tw:Play()
    return tw
end

local function kp(t, v) return NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(t, v) end
local function corner(r) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }) end
local function pad(l, t, r, b)
    return new("UIPadding", {
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or l), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or t),
    })
end

local function accentize(obj, prop)
    table.insert(AccentItems, { obj, prop })
    obj[prop] = Library.Accent
end

local function isPress(i)
    return i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end
local function isMove(i)
    return i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end
-- Vị trí trong hệ toạ độ ScreenGui (IgnoreGuiInset = true) -> cộng thêm inset
local function screenPos(i)
    return Vector2.new(i.Position.X, i.Position.Y) + GuiService:GetGuiInset()
end

local function ensureIridAnim()
    if iridConn then return end
    local t0 = os.clock()
    iridConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local rot = (225 + (os.clock() - t0) * 45) % 360
        for i = #Irid, 1, -1 do
            local g = Irid[i]
            if g.Parent then g.Rotation = rot else table.remove(Irid, i) end
        end
    end)
end

-- Viền kính. uniform=true: viền đều kiểu "outline" (hàng, nút); false: sáng 2 góc (cửa sổ)
local function glassStroke(parent, thickness, strength, uniform)
    local s = new("UIStroke", {
        Thickness = thickness or 1.5, Color = WHITE, Transparency = strength or 0.3,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
    })
    local seq = uniform and NumberSequence.new({ kp(0, 0), kp(0.5, 0.4), kp(1, 0) })
        or NumberSequence.new({ kp(0, 0), kp(0.3, 0.6), kp(0.5, 0.92), kp(0.75, 0.6), kp(1, 0.05) })
    new("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Transparency = seq }).Parent = s
    s.Parent = parent
    return s
end

local function glassShine(parent, radius)
    local f = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Shine", BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = parent.ZIndex, Active = false,
    }, { corner(radius) })
    new("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ kp(0, 0.1), kp(0.2, 0.55), kp(0.5, 0.93), kp(0.78, 1), kp(1, 0.6) }),
    }).Parent = f
    f.Parent = parent
    return f
end

-- o.light = true: chỉ nền + viền + bóng (nhẹ, dùng cho hàng/nút nhỏ)
-- o.themed = true: màu nền theo theme
-- o.uniform = true: viền đều
local function glass(parent, props, radius, transparency, o)
    o = o or {}
    local r = radius or 12
    local f = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = transparency or 0.5, BorderSizePixel = 0 })
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do f[k] = v end
    corner(r).Parent = f
    glassStroke(f, o.uniform and 1.3 or 1.5, o.uniform and 0.36 or 0.2, o.uniform)
    glassShine(f, r)
    if not o.light and r >= 16 then
        -- điểm chói như ánh đèn phản chiếu trên mặt kính cong
        local glint = new("Frame", {
            Name = "Glint", BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.78, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.07, 0, 0, 5), Size = UDim2.new(0.46, 0, 0, math.max(6, math.floor(r * 0.34))),
            Active = false, ZIndex = f.ZIndex,
        }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
        new("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ kp(0, 0.15), kp(0.55, 0.7), kp(1, 1) }) }).Parent = glint
        glint.Parent = f
    end
    if not o.light then
        -- đáy tối nhẹ: tách lớp kính
        local depth = new("Frame", {
            Name = "Depth", BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Active = false,
        }, { corner(r) })
        new("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ kp(0, 1), kp(0.6, 1), kp(1, 0.82) }) }).Parent = depth
        depth.Parent = f
        -- viền khúc xạ trong, màu cầu vồng xoay
        if r >= 16 then
            local rim = new("Frame", {
                Name = "Rim", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -6), Active = false,
            }, { corner(r - 3) })
            local st = new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1.3, Color = WHITE, Transparency = 0.4, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border })
            local ig = new("UIGradient", {
                Rotation = 225,
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(140, 230, 255)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, WHITE),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 235)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 150)),
                }),
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ kp(0, 0.1), kp(0.35, 0.85), kp(0.65, 0.85), kp(1, 0.1) }),
            })
            ig.Parent = st
            table.insert(Irid, ig)
            ensureIridAnim()
            st.Parent = rim
            rim.Parent = f
        end
    end
    if not o.light and r >= 20 then
        -- tán sắc: hai viền đỏ/xanh lệch nhau 1px như mép thấu kính thật
        for _, c in ipairs({ { Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 130), 1 }, { Color3.fromRGB(80, 210, 255), -1 } }) do
            local ch = new("Frame", {
                Name = "Chroma", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c[2], 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Active = false,
            }, { corner(r) })
            new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1, Color = c[1], Transparency = 0.8, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }).Parent = ch
            ch.Parent = f
        end
    end
    f:SetAttribute("GT", f.BackgroundTransparency)
    table.insert(GlassItems, {
        f = f, base = f.BackgroundColor3, themed = o.themed,
        clear = math.min(0.98, f.BackgroundTransparency + 0.3), tinted = math.max(0.2, f.BackgroundTransparency - 0.3),
    })
    f.Parent = parent
    return f
end

local function applyGlass(animate)
    local level = Library.GlassLevel
    local alive = {}
    for _, it in ipairs(GlassItems) do
        if it.f and it.f.Parent then
            table.insert(alive, it)
            local t = it.clear + (it.tinted - it.clear) * level
            local base = it.themed and Library.Tint or it.base
            local col
            if level < 0.5 then col = base:Lerp(WHITE, (0.5 - level) * 0.6)
            else col = base:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(24, 28, 38), (level - 0.5) * 1.4) end
            it.f:SetAttribute("GT", t)
            if animate then tween(it.f, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = t, BackgroundColor3 = col })
            else it.f.BackgroundTransparency = t; it.f.BackgroundColor3 = col end
        end
    end
    GlassItems = alive
    for _, w in ipairs(Library.Windows) do
        if w.Blur and w.Visible then tween(w.Blur, 0.3, { Size = 14 + level * 14 }) end
    end
end

function Library:SetGlass(level)
    Library.GlassLevel = math.clamp(level or 0.5, 0, 1)
    applyGlass(true)
end

function Library:SetEffects(on)
    Library.Effects = on and true or false
    for _, w in ipairs(Library.Windows) do
        if w.Fx and w.Fx.Parent then w.Fx.Visible = Library.Effects end
    end
end

function Library:SetTheme(name)
    local th = Library.Themes[name]
    if not th then return end
    Library.ThemeName, Library.Tint, Library.Accent = name, th.Tint, th.Accent
    local alive = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(AccentItems) do
        if e[1] and e[1].Parent then table.insert(alive, e); tween(e[1], 0.3, { [e[2]] = th.Accent }) end
    end
    AccentItems = alive
    applyGlass(true)
end

local function gui_parent()
    local ok, h = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
    if ok and h then return h end
    local ok2 = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui"):GetChildren() end)
    if ok2 then return game:GetService("CoreGui") end
    return Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

-- ========== Thông báo ==========
local notifyGui, notifyHolder
function Library:Notify(title, content, duration)
    if not notifyGui or not notifyGui.Parent then
        notifyGui = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "FluentGlassNotify", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 2147483100 })
        notifyGui.Parent = gui_parent()
        notifyHolder = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 12), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(270, 400),
        }, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })
        notifyHolder.Parent = notifyGui
    end
    local card = glass(notifyHolder, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(270, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y }, 20, 0.4, { themed = true })
    card.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 0)
    pad(14, 10).Parent = card
    local list = new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder })
    -- UIListLayout chỉ áp cho 2 nhãn: đặt trong khung chứa riêng
    local body = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 3, Parent = card }, { list })
    new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, LayoutOrder = 1,
        TextColor3 = TEXT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = tostring(title), ZIndex = 3, Parent = body,
    })
    new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextWrapped = true, TextColor3 = SUB,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = tostring(content or ""), ZIndex = 3, Parent = body,
    })
    tween(card, 0.7, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
    local function dismiss()
        if card.Parent then
            tween(card, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 0) })
            task.wait(0.32)
            card:Destroy()
        end
    end
    if duration == 0 then
        local x = new("TextButton", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, -4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22),
            Text = "✕", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = SUB, ZIndex = 6, Parent = card,
        })
        x.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() task.spawn(dismiss) end)
    else
        task.delay(duration or 4, dismiss)
    end
end

-- ========== Panel dropdown nổi ==========
local activePanel
local function closePanel()
    if activePanel then
        local p = activePanel
        activePanel = nil
        p()
    end
end

local function openPanel(screen, anchor, options, selected, onPick)
    closePanel()
    local catcher = new("TextButton", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 60, Parent = screen,
    })
    local ap, as, vp = anchor.AbsolutePosition, anchor.AbsoluteSize, screen.AbsoluteSize
    local pw = math.max(as.X, 220)
    local ph = math.min(64 + #options * 40, 290)
    local x = math.clamp(ap.X + as.X - pw, 8, math.max(8, vp.X - pw - 8))
    local y = ap.Y + as.Y + 8
    if y + ph > vp.Y - 8 then y = math.max(8, ap.Y - ph - 8) end

    local panel = glass(screen, {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(as.X, as.Y), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ap.X, ap.Y), ZIndex = 61, ClipsDescendants = true,
    }, 22, 0.38, { themed = true })
    applyGlass(false)

    local sbox = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.9, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 34), ZIndex = 63, Parent = panel,
    }, { corner(17) })
    glassStroke(sbox, 1.2, 0.45, true)
    new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 34), Text = "⌕", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 17, TextColor3 = SUB, ZIndex = 64, Parent = sbox })
    local search = new("TextBox", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0), ClearTextOnFocus = false, Text = "",
        PlaceholderText = "Search...", PlaceholderColor3 = SUB, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = TEXT,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 64, Parent = sbox,
    })

    local list = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 52), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -58), BorderSizePixel = 0,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4, ZIndex = 63, Parent = panel,
    }, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })

    local function build(filter)
        for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end end
        for idx, opt in ipairs(options) do
            local name = tostring(opt)
            if filter == "" or string.find(string.lower(name), filter, 1, true) then
                local on = (opt == selected)
                local b = new("TextButton", {
                    BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.82 or 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 36), LayoutOrder = idx,
                    AutoButtonColor = false, Text = name, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = TEXT,
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 64, Parent = list,
                }, { corner(18), pad(16, 0, 30, 0) })
                if on then
                    glassStroke(b, 1.3, 0.35, true)
                    local bar = new("Frame", { BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18), ZIndex = 65, Parent = b }, { corner(2) })
                    accentize(bar, "BackgroundColor3")
                    new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Text = "●", TextSize = 10, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextColor3 = TEXT, ZIndex = 65, Parent = b })
                end
                b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not on then tween(b, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.9 }) end end)
                b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not on then tween(b, 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end end)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    selected = opt
                    onPick(opt)
                    closePanel()
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    build("")
    search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() build(string.lower(search.Text)) end)

    tween(panel, 0.8, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pw, ph), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)

    activePanel = function()
        catcher:Destroy()
        tween(panel, 0.25, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(as.X, as.Y), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ap.X, ap.Y), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        task.delay(0.27, function() panel:Destroy() end)
    end
    catcher.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closePanel)
end

-- ========== Cửa sổ ==========
function Library:CreateWindow(cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}
    if cfg.Theme then Library:SetTheme(cfg.Theme) end
    local Window = { Tabs = {}, Visible = true, Maximized = false }

    local screen = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "FluentGlassLiquid", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 2147483000, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling })
    screen.Parent = gui_parent()
    Window.Gui = screen

    local blur
    if cfg.Blur ~= false then
        blur = new("BlurEffect", { Name = "FluentGlassBlur", Size = 0 })
        blur.Parent = Lighting
        Window.Blur = blur
    end

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(900, 600)
    local w = math.min(cfg.Width or 660, vp.X - 24)
    local h = math.min(cfg.Height or 410, vp.Y - 48)
    local compact = w < 540
    local sideW = compact and 56 or 190
    Library.ContentWidth = w - sideW - 56
    local curW, curH = w, h

    local main = glass(screen, {
        Name = "Main", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h),
    }, 28, 0.42, { themed = true })
    applyGlass(false)
    Window.Main = main

    main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 0.9, h * 0.9)
    tween(main, 0.9, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
    if blur then tween(blur, 0.6, { Size = 14 + Library.GlassLevel * 14 }) end

    -- Vạch sáng mép trên + đèn rọi bám theo ngón tay
    local topLine = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 2), Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 1), Parent = main })
    new("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ kp(0, 1), kp(0.5, 0.15), kp(1, 1) }) }).Parent = topLine

    local glowClip = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true, Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -28), Parent = main })
    -- Lớp "chất lỏng": cực quang trôi chậm + bong bóng nổi (tắt bằng Library:SetEffects(false))
    local fx = new("Frame", { Name = "Fx", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Visible = Library.Effects, Parent = glowClip })
    Window.Fx = fx
    local blobs, bubbles = {}, {}
    for k = 1, 3 do
        local root = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = fx })
        for _, d in ipairs({ { 300, 0.95 }, { 190, 0.93 }, { 100, 0.91 } }) do
            local c = new("Frame", {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d[1], d[1]), BorderSizePixel = 0,
                BackgroundTransparency = d[2], Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = root,
            }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
            accentize(c, "BackgroundColor3")
        end
        blobs[k] = root
    end
    for k = 1, 8 do
        local sz = 12 + (k * 7) % 28
        local b = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.92, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = fx,
        }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
        new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1, Color = WHITE, Transparency = 0.5, Parent = b })
        bubbles[k] = { f = b, x = (k * 0.137) % 1, y = (k * 0.31) % 1, sp = 0.02 + 0.012 * (k % 4), ph = k * 1.7 }
    end
    local fxConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not Library.Effects or not main.Visible then return end
        local t, sz = os.clock(), glowClip.AbsoluteSize
        for k, root in ipairs(blobs) do
            root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sz.X * (0.5 + 0.42 * math.sin(t * 0.23 * k + k)), sz.Y * (0.5 + 0.42 * math.cos(t * 0.19 * k + k * 2)))
        end
        for _, bb in ipairs(bubbles) do
            bb.y = bb.y - bb.sp * dt
            if bb.y < -0.1 then bb.y = 1.1 end
            bb.f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sz.X * (bb.x + 0.03 * math.sin(t + bb.ph)), sz.Y * bb.y)
        end
    end)
    local glowRoot = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = glowClip })
    for _, d in ipairs({ { 320, 0.968 }, { 210, 0.958 }, { 120, 0.945 }, { 56, 0.925 } }) do
        new("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d[1], d[1]), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE,
            BackgroundTransparency = d[2], BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = glowRoot,
        }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
    end
    local glowTarget = Vector2.new(w * 0.3, h * 0.25)
    local glowPos = glowTarget
    local function setGlow(i) glowTarget = screenPos(i) - glowClip.AbsolutePosition end
    local glowIn1 = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i) if main.Visible and isMove(i) then setGlow(i) end end)
    local glowIn2 = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if main.Visible and isPress(i) then setGlow(i) end end)
    local glowConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        glowPos = glowPos:Lerp(glowTarget, math.clamp(dt * 8, 0, 1))
        glowRoot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(glowPos.X, glowPos.Y)
    end)
    local sheen = main:FindFirstChild("Shine") and main.Shine:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
    local sheenConn
    if sheen then
        local t0 = os.clock()
        sheenConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local t = os.clock() - t0
            sheen.Rotation = 90 + math.sin(t * 0.6) * 18
            sheen.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * 0.4) * 0.12, 0)
        end)
    end

    -- Thanh tiêu đề
    local top = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48), ZIndex = 4, Parent = main })
    local logo = new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 48), Text = "◈", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 20, ZIndex = 5, Parent = top })
    accentize(logo, "TextColor3")
    local titleLbl = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -210, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 16,
        TextColor3 = TEXT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = cfg.Title or "FluentGlass", ZIndex = 5, Parent = top,
    })
    if cfg.SubTitle and cfg.SubTitle ~= "" then
        titleLbl.RichText = true
        titleLbl.Text = (cfg.Title or "FluentGlass") .. '  <font color="rgb(170,176,188)" weight="regular">| ' .. cfg.SubTitle .. "</font>"
    end
    -- Nút cửa sổ kiểu Fluent:  –   ▢   ✕   (vẽ bằng khung, không phụ thuộc font)
    local function winBtn(kind, xOff)
        local isClose = (kind == "close")
        local b = new("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3 = isClose and Color3.fromRGB(232, 72, 85) or WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, xOff, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 30), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 6, Parent = top,
        }, { corner(8) })
        local parts = {}   -- {đối tượng, thuộc tính màu}
        local function stroke(parent, thick)
            local st = new("UIStroke", { Thickness = thick, Color = SUB, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = parent })
            table.insert(parts, { st, "Color" })
        end
        local function line(rot)
            local f = new("Frame", {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(kind == "min" and 12 or 15, 2),
                BackgroundColor3 = SUB, BorderSizePixel = 0, Rotation = rot or 0, ZIndex = 7, Active = false, Parent = b,
            }, { corner(1) })
            table.insert(parts, { f, "BackgroundColor3" })
        end
        if kind == "min" then
            line(0)
        elseif kind == "max" then
            local sq = new("Frame", {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 11),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 7, Active = false, Parent = b,
            }, { corner(2) })
            stroke(sq, 1.6)
        else
            line(45); line(-45)
        end
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            tween(b, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = isClose and 0.15 or 0.88 })
            for _, p in ipairs(parts) do tween(p[1], 0.15, { [p[2]] = isClose and WHITE or TEXT }) end
        end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            tween(b, 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
            for _, p in ipairs(parts) do tween(p[1], 0.2, { [p[2]] = SUB }) end
        end)
        return b
    end
    local closeBtn = winBtn("close", -8)
    local maxBtn = winBtn("max", -50)
    local minBtn = winBtn("min", -92)
    new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.85, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 48), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), ZIndex = 4, Parent = main })

    -- Kéo cửa sổ (nghiêng nhẹ như thạch)
    do
        local dragging, startPos, startInput, lastX, activeInput
        local function clampToScreen()
            local sz, ap, ss = main.AbsoluteSize, main.AbsolutePosition, screen.AbsoluteSize
            local dx, dy = 0, 0
            local maxX, maxY = math.max(0, ss.X - sz.X), math.max(0, ss.Y - sz.Y)
            if ap.X < 0 then dx = -ap.X elseif ap.X > maxX then dx = maxX - ap.X end
            if ap.Y < 0 then dy = -ap.Y elseif ap.Y > maxY then dy = maxY - ap.Y end
            if dx ~= 0 or dy ~= 0 then
                local p = main.Position
                tween(main, 0.35, { Position = UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset + dx, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset + dy) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
            end
        end
        local function endDrag()
            if not dragging then return end
            dragging, activeInput = false, nil
            tween(main, 0.7, { Rotation = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
            clampToScreen()
        end
        top.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if isPress(i) and not dragging then
                dragging, activeInput, startPos, startInput, lastX = true, i, main.Position, i.Position, i.Position.X
                -- ngón tay nhả là dừng kéo, kể cả khi ScrollingFrame nuốt mất sự kiện InputEnded
                local conn
                conn = i.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
                        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                        if activeInput == i then endDrag() end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and isMove(i) and (i == activeInput or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
                local d = i.Position - startInput
                main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                local vx = i.Position.X - lastX
                lastX = i.Position.X
                main.Rotation = main.Rotation + (math.clamp(vx * 0.3, -2, 2) - main.Rotation) * 0.3
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i == activeInput or isPress(i)) then endDrag() end
        end)
        -- lưới an toàn: cửa sổ không bao giờ được nghiêng khi không kéo (nghiêng làm mất cắt nội dung)
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not dragging and math.abs(main.Rotation) > 0.05 and not main:GetAttribute("Settling") then
                main:SetAttribute("Settling", true)
                tween(main, 0.4, { Rotation = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
                task.delay(0.45, function() main:SetAttribute("Settling", nil); if not dragging then main.Rotation = 0 end end)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Thanh bên: ô tìm kiếm + tab
    local side = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 58), Size = UDim2.new(0, sideW, 1, -70), ZIndex = 3, Parent = main })
    local sbox = glass(side, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), ZIndex = 4 }, 19, 0.92, { light = true, uniform = true })
    new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(compact and 0 or 12, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(compact and 56 or 20, 38), Text = "⌕", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = SUB, ZIndex = 6, Parent = sbox })
    local searchBox = new("TextBox", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0), ClearTextOnFocus = false, Text = "",
        PlaceholderText = "Search...", PlaceholderColor3 = SUB, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = TEXT,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Visible = not compact, ZIndex = 6, Parent = sbox,
    })
    local tabsArea = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 48), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -48), ZIndex = 3, Parent = side })
    local sideList = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ScrollBarThickness = 0, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 4, Parent = tabsArea,
    })
    local tabHolder = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 4, Parent = sideList,
    }, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }) })
    local lens = glass(sideList, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), ZIndex = 3 }, 20, 0.8, { uniform = true })
    lens.Visible = false

    local pages = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sideW + 26, 58), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(sideW + 38), 1, -70), ZIndex = 3, ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = main,
    })

    -- Tìm kiếm: lọc các hàng của mọi trang
    searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local q = string.lower(searchBox.Text)
        for _, t in ipairs(Window.Tabs) do
            for _, c in ipairs(t.Page:GetChildren()) do
                local key = c:GetAttribute("SearchKey")
                if key then c.Visible = (q == "") or (string.find(key, q, 1, true) ~= nil) end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Nút tròn nổi khi thu nhỏ
    local orb = glass(screen, { Name = "Orb", Visible = false, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 120), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 52) }, 26, 0.45, { themed = true })
    applyGlass(false)
    -- Ảnh trên nút tròn nổi (đổi bằng cfg.OrbImage = "rbxassetid://...")
    new("ImageLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10),
        Image = cfg.OrbImage or "rbxassetid://127729348105692", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ZIndex = 5, Active = false, Parent = orb,
    }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
    local orbBtn = new("TextButton", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = "", ZIndex = 6, Parent = orb })

    function Window:Toggle(state)
        if state == nil then state = not Window.Visible end
        Window.Visible = state
        closePanel()
        if state then
            main.Visible = true; orb.Visible = false
            main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(curW * 0.85, curH * 0.85)
            tween(main, 0.9, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(curW, curH) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
            if blur then tween(blur, 0.5, { Size = 14 + Library.GlassLevel * 14 }) end
        else
            if blur then tween(blur, 0.3, { Size = 0 }) end
            tween(main, 0.25, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(curW * 0.85, curH * 0.85) })
            task.delay(0.22, function() if not Window.Visible then main.Visible = false; orb.Visible = true end end)
        end
    end
    local orbGuard = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if Window.Visible then
            if orb.Visible then orb.Visible = false end
            if not main.Visible then main.Visible = true end
        elseif not main.Visible and not orb.Visible then
            orb.Visible = true
        end
    end)
    function Window:Maximize()
        Window.Maximized = not Window.Maximized
        if Window.Maximized then
            curW, curH = screen.AbsoluteSize.X - 24, screen.AbsoluteSize.Y - 24
            tween(main, 0.7, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(curW, curH), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
        else
            curW, curH = w, h
            tween(main, 0.7, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
        end
    end
    function Window:Destroy()
        closePanel()
        for _, c in ipairs({ glowConn, glowIn1, glowIn2, sheenConn, fxConn, orbGuard }) do if c then c:Disconnect() end end
        if blur then blur:Destroy() end
        screen:Destroy()
    end
    minBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Window:Toggle(false) end)
    maxBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Window:Maximize() end)
    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Window:Destroy() end)
    orbBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Window:Toggle(true) end)
    do
        local drag, sp, si, orbInput
        orbBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if isPress(i) and not drag then
                drag, sp, si, orbInput = true, orb.Position, i.Position, i
                local conn
                conn = i.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
                        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                        if orbInput == i then drag, orbInput = false, nil end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if drag and isMove(i) and (i == orbInput or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
                local d = i.Position - si
                orb.Position = UDim2.new(sp.X.Scale, sp.X.Offset + d.X, sp.Y.Scale, sp.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if drag and (i == orbInput or isPress(i)) then drag, orbInput = false, nil end
        end)
    end

    -- ========== Tab ==========
    local currentIndex, lensTop, lensToken = nil, 0, 0
    -- vòng gợn nước toả ra quanh tab vừa chọn
    local ripple = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Ripple", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), ZIndex = 2, Active = false,
        Parent = sideList,
    }, { corner(20) })
    local rippleStroke = new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 2, Color = WHITE, Transparency = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = ripple })

    local function playRipple(top)
        if not Library.Effects then return end
        ripple.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, top)
        ripple.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        ripple.Visible = true
        rippleStroke.Transparency = 0.25
        tween(ripple, 0.6, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-6, top - 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, 12, 0, 52) })
        tween(rippleStroke, 0.6, { Transparency = 1 })
    end

    local function selectTab(tab)
        closePanel()
        local newIndex = table.find(Window.Tabs, tab) or 1
        local first = (currentIndex == nil)
        local dir = (currentIndex and newIndex < currentIndex) and -1 or 1
        currentIndex = newIndex
        for _, t in ipairs(Window.Tabs) do
            local on = (t == tab)
            if on and not t.Page.Visible and not first and Library.Effects then
                -- nội dung trượt vào theo chiều đổi tab
                t.Page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, dir * 24)
                tween(t.Page, 0.45, { Position = UDim2.new() }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            end
            t.Page.Visible = on
            tween(t.Label, 0.25, { TextColor3 = on and TEXT or SUB })
            tween(t.Bar, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 })
        end
        lensToken = lensToken + 1
        local token = lensToken
        task.defer(function()
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if not tab.Button.Parent or not tabHolder.Parent then return end
            local newTop = tab.Button.AbsolutePosition.Y - tabHolder.AbsolutePosition.Y
            if not lens.Visible then
                lens.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, newTop)
                lens.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
                lens.Visible = true
                lensTop = newTop
                return
            end
            local oldTop = lensTop
            lensTop = newTop
            if oldTop == newTop then return end
            if not Library.Effects then
                lens.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, newTop)
                lens.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
                return
            end
            -- giọt nước: mép đi trước kéo dãn ra, mép sau bám theo rồi co lại
            lens.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, oldTop)
            lens.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
            local reach = math.abs(newTop - oldTop) + 40
            if dir > 0 then
                tween(lens, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, reach) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad).Completed:Connect(function()
                    if token ~= lensToken then return end
                    playRipple(newTop)
                    tween(lens, 0.55, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, newTop), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                end)
            else
                tween(lens, 0.2, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, newTop), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, reach) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad).Completed:Connect(function()
                    if token ~= lensToken then return end
                    playRipple(newTop)
                    tween(lens, 0.55, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    function Window:SelectTab(index)
        local t = Window.Tabs[index]
        if t then selectTab(t) end
    end

    function Window:AddTab(name, icon)
        local Tab = {}
        local btn = new("TextButton", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
            LayoutOrder = #Window.Tabs + 1, ZIndex = 5, Parent = tabHolder,
        })
        local bar = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -10), ZIndex = 7, Parent = btn }, { corner(2) })
        accentize(bar, "BackgroundColor3")
        bar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        local label = new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = SUB, ZIndex = 6, Parent = btn,
            Text = compact and (icon or string.sub(name, 1, 1)) or ((icon and (icon .. "   ") or "") .. name),
            TextXAlignment = compact and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        })
        if not compact then pad(18, 0, 4, 0).Parent = label end

        local page = new("ScrollingFrame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3,
            ScrollBarImageColor3 = WHITE, ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.5, ZIndex = 4, Parent = pages,
        }, { new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }), pad(2, 0, 8, 10) })
        local order = 0
        local function nextOrder() order = order + 1; return order end
        new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 24,
            TextColor3 = TEXT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = name, ZIndex = 5, Parent = page,
        })

        Tab.Button, Tab.Label, Tab.Bar, Tab.Page = btn, label, bar, page
        table.insert(Window.Tabs, Tab)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectTab(Tab) end)
        if #Window.Tabs == 1 then selectTab(Tab) end

        -- Hàng (card) dạng viên thuốc có viền sáng, tiêu đề + mô tả
        local function card(height, title, desc, reserve, descLines)
            local right = (reserve or 0) + 28
            local avail = math.max((Library.ContentWidth or 260) - right, 60)
            local function linesFor(text, charW)
                local n = 0
                for part in (tostring(text) .. "\n"):gmatch("(.-)\n") do
                    n = n + math.max(1, math.ceil(#part / math.max(math.floor(avail / charW), 6)))
                end
                return n
            end
            local tLines = math.min(linesFor(title, 8), 3)
            local dLines = desc and (descLines or math.min(linesFor(desc, 6.4), 6)) or 0
            local need = 18 + tLines * 18 + (desc and (4 + dLines * 15) or 0)
            height = math.max(height, need)
            local r = glass(page, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ZIndex = 5 }, 20, 0.93, { light = true, uniform = true })
            r:SetAttribute("SearchKey", string.lower(tostring(title) .. " " .. tostring(desc or "")))
            new("TextLabel", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18, desc and 9 or 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -right, 0, desc and (tLines * 18) or math.min(height, 48)),
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = TEXT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextYAlignment = desc and Enum.TextYAlignment.Top or Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                TextWrapped = true, Text = tostring(title), ZIndex = 7, Parent = r,
            })
            if desc then
                new("TextLabel", {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 9 + tLines * 18 + 4), Size = UDim2.new(1, -right, 0, dLines * 15),
                    Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = SUB, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true, Text = tostring(desc), ZIndex = 7, Parent = r,
                })
            end
            r.ClipsDescendants = true
            new("Frame", {
                Name = "RowGlow", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 170), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = false, ZIndex = 6, Parent = r,
            }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
            local scale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = r })
            r.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(scale, 0.35, { Scale = 1.012 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
            r.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(scale, 0.35, { Scale = 1 }) end)
            return r, scale
        end

        -- Lớp bấm: ép đàn hồi + gợn nước
        local function clickable(r, scale, onClick)
            r.ClipsDescendants = true
            local click = new("TextButton", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = "", ZIndex = 9, Parent = r })
            local rg = r:FindFirstChild("RowGlow")
            if rg then
                click.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(rg, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.88 }) end)
                click.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(rg, 0.35, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end)
                click.MouseMoved:Connect(function(x, y)
                    local p = Vector2.new(x, y) + GuiService:GetGuiInset() - r.AbsolutePosition
                    tween(rg, 0.18, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p.X, p.Y) })
                end)
            end
            click.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if not isPress(i) then return end
                tween(scale, 0.12, { Scale = 0.965 })
                local p = screenPos(i) - r.AbsolutePosition
                local rip = new("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Active = false, ZIndex = 8, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p.X, p.Y), Parent = r,
                }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
                tween(rip, 0.6, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(r.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.6, r.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.6), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
                task.delay(0.65, function() rip:Destroy() end)
                task.delay(0.12, function()
                    local rip2 = new("Frame", {
                        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = false, ZIndex = 8,
                        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p.X, p.Y), Parent = r,
                    }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }), new("UIStroke", { Thickness = 2, Color = WHITE, Transparency = 0.4 }) })
                    tween(rip2, 0.7, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(r.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.3, r.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.3) })
                    tween(rip2.UIStroke, 0.7, { Transparency = 1 })
                    task.delay(0.75, function() rip2:Destroy() end)
                end)
            end)
            click.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if isPress(i) then tween(scale, 0.6, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic) end
            end)
            click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onClick)
            return click
        end

        function Tab:AddSection(text)
            new("TextLabel", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 20,
                TextColor3 = TEXT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = tostring(text), ZIndex = 5, Parent = page,
            })
        end

        function Tab:AddLabel(a, desc)
            local o = type(a) == "table" and a or { Title = a, Description = desc }
            local r = card(o.Description and 60 or 48, o.Title, o.Description, 0, o.Lines)
            local titleLabel, descLabel
            for _, child in ipairs(r:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    if not titleLabel then titleLabel = child elseif not descLabel then descLabel = child end
                end
            end
            return {
                Set = function(_, t) if titleLabel then titleLabel.Text = tostring(t) end end,
                SetDesc = function(_, t) if descLabel then descLabel.Text = tostring(t) end end,
            }
        end

        function Tab:AddButton(a, cb, desc)
            local o = type(a) == "table" and a or { Title = a, Callback = cb, Description = desc }
            local r, scale = card(o.Description and 60 or 48, o.Title, o.Description, 30)
            new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), Text = "➤", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = TEXT, ZIndex = 7, Parent = r })
            clickable(r, scale, function() if o.Callback then task.spawn(o.Callback) end end)
        end

        function Tab:AddToggle(a, default, cb, desc)
            local o = type(a) == "table" and a or { Title = a, Default = default, Callback = cb, Description = desc }
            local state = o.Default and true or false
            local r, scale = card(o.Description and 60 or 48, o.Title, o.Description, 56)
            local track = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 26), BackgroundTransparency = 0.85, ZIndex = 7, Parent = r }, { corner(13) })
            accentize(track, "BackgroundColor3")
            local ts = glassStroke(track, 1.4, 0.3, true)
            local knob = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3), ZIndex = 8, Parent = track }, { corner(10) })
            glassStroke(knob, 1.2, 0.5, true)
            local api = {}
            local function render(anim)
                local pos = state and UDim2.fromOffset(25, 3) or UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3)
                local bgT = state and 0.1 or 0.85
                if not anim then knob.Position = pos; track.BackgroundTransparency = bgT; return end
                tween(track, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = bgT })
                tween(knob, 0.14, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 20) })   -- giãn như giọt nước
                task.delay(0.1, function() tween(knob, 0.55, { Position = pos, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic) end)
            end
            render(false)
            function api:Set(v, silent) state = v and true or false; render(true); if o.Callback and not silent then task.spawn(o.Callback, state) end end
            function api:Get() return state end
            clickable(r, scale, function() api:Set(not state) end)
            return api
        end

        function Tab:AddSlider(a, min, max, default, cb, desc)
            local o = type(a) == "table" and a or { Title = a, Min = min, Max = max, Default = default, Callback = cb, Description = desc }
            local lo, hi = o.Min or 0, o.Max or 100
            local value = math.clamp(o.Default or lo, lo, hi)
            local height = o.Description and 76 or 64
            local r = card(height, o.Title, o.Description, 70)
            if o.Description then r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, r.Size.Y.Offset + 16) end
            height = r.Size.Y.Offset
            local mult = 10 ^ (o.Rounding or 0)
            local val = new("TextLabel", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 22),
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ZIndex = 7, Parent = r,
            })
            accentize(val, "TextColor3")
            local bar = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.75, Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, height - 20), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 6), ZIndex = 7, Parent = r }, { corner(3) })
            local fill = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), ZIndex = 8, Parent = bar }, { corner(3) })
            accentize(fill, "BackgroundColor3")
            local knob = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 9, Parent = bar }, { corner(13) })
            glassStroke(knob, 1.5, 0.2, true)
            local api = {}
            local function set(v, silent)
                v = math.clamp(math.floor(v * mult + 0.5) / mult, lo, hi)
                value = v
                local a2 = (v - lo) / math.max(hi - lo, 1)
                fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(a2, 1)
                knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(a2, 0.5)
                val.Text = tostring(v)
                if o.Callback and not silent then task.spawn(o.Callback, v) end
            end
            set(value, true)
            function api:Set(v, silent) set(v, silent) end
            function api:Get() return value end
            local dragging = false
            local function fromX(x) set(lo + math.clamp((x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / bar.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1) * (hi - lo)) end
            bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if isPress(i) then
                    dragging = true; page.ScrollingEnabled = false; fromX(i.Position.X)
                    tween(knob, 0.35, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 22) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i) if dragging and isMove(i) then fromX(i.Position.X) end end)
            UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if isPress(i) and dragging then
                    dragging = false; page.ScrollingEnabled = true
                    tween(knob, 0.6, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
                end
            end)
            return api
        end

        function Tab:AddDropdown(a, options, default, cb, desc)
            local o = type(a) == "table" and a or { Title = a, Options = options, Default = default, Callback = cb, Description = desc }
            local opts = o.Options or {}
            local selected = o.Default
            local r, scale = card(o.Description and 60 or 48, o.Title, o.Description, 190)
            local pill = glass(r, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(176, 32), ZIndex = 7 }, 16, 0.9, { light = true, uniform = true })
            local cur = new("TextLabel", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = TEXT,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = tostring(selected or "Chọn..."), ZIndex = 8, Parent = pill,
            })
            new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 32), Text = "›", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = TEXT, ZIndex = 8, Parent = pill })
            local api = {}
            function api:Set(v, silent) selected = v; cur.Text = tostring(v ~= nil and v or "Chọn..."); if o.Callback and not silent then task.spawn(o.Callback, v) end end
            function api:Get() return selected end
            function api:SetOptions(list) opts = list end
            clickable(r, scale, function()
                openPanel(screen, pill, opts, selected, function(v) api:Set(v) end)
            end)
            return api
        end

        function Tab:AddInput(a, placeholder, cb, desc)
            local o = type(a) == "table" and a or { Title = a, Placeholder = placeholder, Callback = cb, Description = desc }
            local r = card(o.Description and 60 or 48, o.Title, o.Description, 200)
            local pill = glass(r, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(186, 32), ZIndex = 7 }, 16, 0.9, { light = true, uniform = true })
            local box = new("TextBox", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0), ClearTextOnFocus = false, Text = "",
                PlaceholderText = o.Placeholder or "", PlaceholderColor3 = SUB, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = TEXT,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 8, Parent = pill,
            })
            local st = pill:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            box.Focused:Connect(function() tween(st, 0.2, { Transparency = 0, Thickness = 2 }) end)
            box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
                tween(st, 0.2, { Transparency = 0.45, Thickness = 1.3 })
                if o.Callback then task.spawn(o.Callback, box.Text, enter) end
            end)
            return { Set = function(_, t) box.Text = tostring(t) end, Get = function() return box.Text end }
        end

        return Tab
    end

    table.insert(Library.Windows, Window)
    return Window
end

return Library

	if bp and bp:FindFirstChild(toolName) then
		return true
	end

	if char and char:FindFirstChild(toolName) then
		return true
		-- [Source Leak] Leaked By SL | Join Discord: https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK
	end
	return false
end

local function GetInventory(forceRefresh)
	local genv = getgenv()
	local cache = genv.RealKidInventoryCache
	if not forceRefresh then
		if
			type(cache) == "table"
			and type(cache.Items) == "table"
			and type(cache.ByName) == "table"
			and type(cache.ByType) == "table"
			and type(cache.Materials) == "table"
			and (tick() - (cache.Time or 0) < 3)
		then
			return cache.Items
		end
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local mods = genv.RealKidInventoryModules
		if type(mods) ~= "table" then
			mods = {
				Service = require(ReplicatedStorage.ItemReplicationService),
				Config = require(ReplicatedStorage.ItemConfig),
				Keys = require(ReplicatedStorage.ItemReplicationService.KEYS),
			}
			genv.RealKidInventoryModules = mods
		end
		local items, byName, byType, materials = {}, {}, {}, {}
		-- More paid scripts leaked for free in SL Discord: https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK
		for _, bag in pairs(mods.Service:GetClientInventoryIterator()) do
			for itemId, rawItem in pairs(bag) do
				local cfg = mods.Config[itemId]
				if type(cfg) == "table" then
					local name = cfg.Name or tostring(itemId)
					local itemType = cfg.Type or ""
					local count = tonumber(rawItem[mods.Keys.Count]) or 1
					local mastery = tonumber(rawItem[mods.Keys.Mastery]) or 0
					local entry = {
						Id = itemId,
						Name = name,
						DisplayName = cfg.DisplayName or name,
						Type = itemType,
						Count = count,
						Mastery = mastery,
						MasteryRequirements = cfg.MasteryRequirements,
						Rarity = cfg.Rarity or 0,
						Value = cfg.Value,
						Raw = rawItem,
						Config = cfg,
					}
					items[#items + 1] = entry
					byName[name] = entry
					if not byType[itemType] then
						byType[itemType] = {}
					end
					byType[itemType][name] = entry
					if itemType == "Material" then
						materials[name] = count
					end
					-- Leaked By SL | Source Leak [https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK]
				end
			end
		end
		genv.RealKidInventoryCache = {
			Time = tick(),
			Items = items,
			ByName = byName,
			ByType = byType,
			Materials = materials,
		}
		return items
	end)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" then
		return result
	end
	return (type(cache) == "table" and type(cache.Items) == "table") and cache.Items or {}
end

end

local function TweenToTarget(targetCFrame)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		local char = plr and plr.Character
		if not char then
			return
		end
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hum or not hrp or hum.Health <= 0 then
			return
		end
		if not targetCFrame then
			return
		end
		local destPos = targetCFrame.Position
		local dist = (destPos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
		-- SL | Source Leak [https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK] - Join SL for more script leaks
		if dist <= 250 then
			if activeTween then
				pcall(function() activeTween:Cancel() end)
				activeTween = nil
			end
			hrp.CFrame = targetCFrame
			return
		end
		local speed = tonumber(getgenv().TweenSpeed) or 160
		local info = TweenInfo.new(dist / speed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
		activeTween = TweenService:Create(hrp, info, { CFrame = targetCFrame })
		activeTween:Play()
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("topos error:", err)
	end
end

local function AimbotNamecallHook(self, ...)

	local args = { ... }

	local shouldRedirect = (
		method == "FireServer"
		and self.Name == "RemoteEvent"
		and aimCF
		and tostring(aimCF.X) ~= "nan"
		and (
			(_G.FarmMastery_G and not SoulGuitar)
			-- Source leaked by SL | Get more free hub sources at https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK
			or _G.FarmMastery_Dev
			or _G.FarmBlazeEM
			or _G.Prehis_Skills
			or _G.SeaBeast1
			or _G.FishBoat
			or _G.PGB
			or _G.Leviathan1
			or _G.AutoTrialRace
			or _G.Defeating
			or _G.Auto_RaceV3
			or _G.AimMethod
		)
	)
	if shouldRedirect then
		if #args == 1 and typeof(args[1]) == "Vector3" then
			args[1] = aimCF.Position
			if originalNamecall then
				return originalNamecall(self, unpack(args))
			end
		elseif #args >= 1 and typeof(args[1]) == "CFrame" then
			args[1] = aimCF
			if originalNamecall then
				return originalNamecall(self, unpack(args))
			end
		end
	end
	if originalNamecall then
		return originalNamecall(self, ...)
	end
end

local lastFarmCheckTick = 0

local prevIsFarming = false

	"AutoFarm", "AutoFarmLevel", "AutoFarmNearest", "AutoFarmBone",
	"AutoFarmEctoplasm", "AutoFarmCakePrince", "AutoFarmBoss", "AutoRaid",

local function OnHeartbeatFarmUpdate()
	getgenv().OnFarm = shouldTween and true or false
	local char = plr and plr.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local now = tick()
	if now - lastFarmCheckTick < 0.1 then
		return
	end
	lastFarmCheckTick = now
	isFarming = false
	if _G.AutoMaterial or NextIs or senth or senth2 then
		isFarming = true
	else
		for _, flagName in ipairs(FARM_GLOBAL_FLAGS) do
			if _G[flagName] then
				isFarming = true
				break
			end
		end
	end
	-- Join SL Discord for more open-source & leaked scripts: https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	if isFarming then
		shouldTween = true
		local inBoat = hum.Sit and (_G.SailBoat_Hydra or _G.SailBoat_Tiki)
		if inBoat and hrp:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
			hrp.BodyClip:Destroy()
		elseif not inBoat and not hrp:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
			local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			bv.Name = "BodyClip"
			bv.Parent = hrp
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
		end
		if not hasFarmHighlight then
			local hl = Instance.new("Highlight", char)
			hl.Name = "highlight"
			hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 191, 255)
			hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			hl.FillTransparency = 0.5
			hl.OutlineTransparency = 0.2
			hasFarmHighlight = true
		end
		for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then
				part.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
		-- Leaked By SL | Source Leak [https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK] | More leaks in our Discord
	else
		shouldTween = false
		if activeTween then
			pcall(function() activeTween:Cancel() end)
			activeTween = nil
		end
		targetTweenPos = Vector3.zero
		if isFarming ~= prevIsFarming then
			for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
					part.CanCollide = true
				end
			end
		end
		if hrp:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
			hrp.BodyClip:Destroy()
		end
		if char:FindFirstChild("highlight") then
			char.highlight:Destroy()
			hasFarmHighlight = false
		end
	end
	prevIsFarming = isFarming
end

local function OnHeartbeatBoatAndTweenUpdate(dt)
	if not shouldTween then
		return
	end
	local char = plr and plr.Character
	--[[ Leaked By SL | Source Leak [https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK] - Join SL Discord ]]
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	local ghost = workspace:FindFirstChild("_TpGhost")
	if ghost and activeTween and (hrp.Position - targetTweenPos).Magnitude > 5 then
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(ghost.Position) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.Position)
	end
end

local function OnSteppedNoClipUpdate()
	if not shouldTween and not isFarming then
		getgenv().NoClip = false
		return
	end
	getgenv().NoClip = true
	local char = plr and plr.Character
	if char then
		for _, desc in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			if desc:IsA("BasePart") then
				desc.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end -- [SL Source Leak] https://discord.gg/zNyHGU6eUK
end

local function SaveConfig(key, value)
	if key ~= nil then
		ConfigData[key] = value
		if type(getgenv().Flags) == "table" then
			getgenv().Flags[key] = value
		end
	end
	pcall(function()
		local json = HttpService:JSONEncode(ConfigData)
		writefile("RealKidHub/save2/" .. Players.LocalPlayer.Name .. ".json", json)
	end)
end

-- Compatibility bridge: map the source's legacy groupbox API onto FluentGlass Liquid.
local function makeCompatWindow(cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}
    local win = Library:CreateWindow({
        Title = cfg.Title or "Legacy Hub",
        SubTitle = cfg.SubTitle or cfg.Desc or "Blox Fruits",
        Theme = "Obsidian White",
        Width = 660, Height = 410, Blur = true,
        OrbImage = cfg.Image or "rbxassetid://127729348105692"
    })
    local originalAddTab = win.AddTab
    function win:AddTab(name, icon)
        if type(name) == "table" then
            icon = name.Icon or icon
            name = name.Title or name.Name or "Tab"
        end
        local tab = originalAddTab(self, tostring(name), icon)
        -- Normalize legacy option names when the source adds controls directly to a tab.
        local rawButton, rawToggle, rawSlider = tab.AddButton, tab.AddToggle, tab.AddSlider
        local rawDropdown, rawInput, rawLabel = tab.AddDropdown, tab.AddInput, tab.AddLabel
        local function normalize(o)
            if type(o) == "table" then
                o.Title = o.Title or o.Name or o.Text or "Control"
                o.Description = o.Description or o.Desc
                o.Options = o.Options or o.Values
            end
            return o
        end
        function tab:AddButton(a, cb, desc)
            if type(a) == "table" then normalize(a) end
            return rawButton(self, a, cb, desc)
        end
        function tab:AddToggle(a, default, cb, desc)
            if type(a) == "table" then normalize(a) end
            return rawToggle(self, a, default, cb, desc)
        end
        function tab:AddSlider(a, min, max, default, cb, desc)
            if type(a) == "table" then normalize(a) end
            return rawSlider(self, a, min, max, default, cb, desc)
        end
        function tab:AddDropdown(a, options, default, cb, desc)
            if type(a) == "table" then normalize(a) end
            return rawDropdown(self, a, options, default, cb, desc)
        end
        function tab:AddInput(a, placeholder, cb, desc)
            if type(a) == "table" then normalize(a) end
            return rawInput(self, a, placeholder, cb, desc)
        end
        function tab:AddLabel(a, desc)
            if type(a) == "table" then normalize(a) end
            return rawLabel(self, a, desc)
        end
        function tab:AddLeftGroupbox(groupName)
            self:AddSection(tostring(groupName or ""))
            local group = {}
            function group:AddLabel(a, desc)
                local title = type(a) == "table" and (a.Title or a.Name or "") or a
                local description = type(a) == "table" and (a.Desc or a.Description) or desc
                local api = tab:AddLabel({Title = tostring(title or ""), Description = description})
                return {
                    SetText = function(_, value) api:Set(value) end,
                    Set = function(_, value) api:Set(value) end,
                    SetDesc = function(_, value) api:SetDesc(value) end
                }
            end
            function group:AddSection(text) return tab:AddSection(text) end
            function group:AddButton(a, cb)
                local o = type(a) == "table" and a or {Title = a, Callback = cb}
                tab:AddButton({Title = o.Title or o.Name or "Button", Description = o.Desc or o.Description,
                    Callback = function() if o.Callback then o.Callback(true) end end})
            end
            function group:AddToggle(a, opts)
                local o
                if type(a) == "table" then o = a else
                    o = type(opts) == "table" and opts or {Default = false}
                    o.Title = o.Title or a
                end
                return tab:AddToggle({Title = o.Title or o.Name or "Toggle", Description = o.Desc or o.Description,
                    Default = o.Default or false, Callback = function(value) if o.Callback then o.Callback(value) end end})
            end
            function group:AddSlider(a, opts)
                local o
                if type(a) == "table" then o = a else
                    o = type(opts) == "table" and opts or {}
                    o.Title = o.Title or a
                end
                return tab:AddSlider({Title = o.Title or o.Name or "Slider", Description = o.Desc or o.Description,
                    Min = o.Min or 0, Max = o.Max or 100, Default = o.Default or o.Min or 0,
                    Rounding = o.Rounding or 0, Callback = function(value) if o.Callback then o.Callback(value) end end})
            end
            function group:AddInput(a, opts, cb)
                local o
                if type(a) == "table" then o = a else
                    o = type(opts) == "table" and opts or {Placeholder = opts, Callback = cb}
                    o.Title = o.Title or a
                end
                return tab:AddInput({Title = o.Title or o.Name or "Input", Description = o.Desc or o.Description,
                    Placeholder = o.Placeholder or "", Callback = function(value) if o.Callback then o.Callback(value) end end})
            end
            function group:AddDropdown(a, opts)
                local o
                if type(a) == "table" then o = a else
                    o = type(opts) == "table" and opts or {}
                    o.Title = o.Title or a
                end
                local values = o.Values or o.Options or {}
                if o.Multi then
                    local selected = {}
                    if type(o.Default) == "table" then
                        for _, v in ipairs(o.Default) do selected[tostring(v)] = true end
                    elseif o.Default ~= nil then selected[tostring(o.Default)] = true end
                    for _, option in ipairs(values) do
                        local value = tostring(option)
                        tab:AddToggle({Title = value, Description = o.Title or o.Name, Default = selected[value] == true,
                            Callback = function(on)
                                selected[value] = on or nil
                                local result = {}
                                for _, candidate in ipairs(values) do if selected[tostring(candidate)] then table.insert(result, candidate) end end
                                if o.Callback then o.Callback(result) end
                            end})
                    end
                    return {Get = function() local result = {}; for _, v in ipairs(values) do if selected[tostring(v)] then table.insert(result, v) end end; return result end}
                end
                local default = o.Default
                if type(default) == "table" then default = default[1] end
                if default == nil then default = values[1] end
                return tab:AddDropdown({Title = o.Title or o.Name or "Dropdown", Description = o.Desc or o.Description,
                    Options = values, Default = default, Callback = function(value) if o.Callback then o.Callback(value) end end})
            end
            return group
        end
        return tab
    end
    function win:MakeTab(arg)
        if type(arg) == "table" then return self:AddTab(arg.Title or arg.Name or "Tab", arg.Icon) end
        return self:AddTab(arg)
    end
    function win:Notify(arg)
        arg = arg or {}
        Library:Notify(arg.Title or "Notice", arg.Content or arg.Desc or arg.Description or "", arg.Duration or 4)
    end
    win.NewMinimizer = function() end
    return win
end

    end
}
